# Skills · T · part 12

815 entries, tech-design-generator to technical-risk-advisor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tech-design-generator**: Generate technical architecture designs for systems and applications. Produces component designs, communication patterns, database choices, API contracts, shared patterns, and detailed flow designs — all as structured markdown docs. Use this skill when the user says /tech-design-generator, 'design the architecture', 'technical design', 'system design', 'how should we architect this', or wants to define the technical structure before implementation. Also trigger when the user shares requirements or a spec and wants to plan the technical approach. ([dorg-atsabari/forge](https://github.com/dorg-atsabari/forge/tree/HEAD/spec-driven-dev/skills/tech-design-generator) · ★1)
